The first stage is a fast pass that can only approve; actions it does not approve are submitted to the second stage, which is the only stage that can conclude an action must be blocked. Adjudication latency therefore differs between tool calls.”；三类动作不经分类器——只读工具（文件读取、搜索、glob）按工具名放行且不评估目标路径因而工作目录外也放行，工作目录内以及用 `/add-dir` 加进来的目录内的写入与编辑直接放行，其余动作全部提交分类器（含写到这些目录之外）；失败关闭逐字为 “When the classifier is unreachable or returns no usable verdict, the action is denied rather than prompted; the message states that this is not a safety verdict on the action, the same action may be retried, and read-only tools are unaffected. When no classifier model is available, switching into `auto` mode is refused.”；熔断逐字为 “When consecutive block verdicts reach the limit, the circuit breaker trips: the next action becomes an authorization prompt carrying the limit warning and the most recent block reason. The mode itself remains available, and the count clears once an action is approved.” 与 “The circuit breaker is the only condition under which a permission verdict produces an authorization prompt in `auto` mode.”，官方没有公布数值上限；`general.defaultPermissionMode` 为 enum、默认 `default`、取值 `default`/`accept_edits`/`plan`/`auto`/`bypass_permissions`/`dont_ask`、不需重启，顶层 `autoMode` 为 object、默认 `{}`、不需重启、子键为 `allow`/`soft_deny`/`environment`（snake_case，与 Qwen 的 `softDeny` 写法不同且没有 hardDeny 对应键），设置参考逐字为 “Auto mode classifier soft-steering rules (`allow`/`soft_deny`/`environment`). Top-level key, read only from user-level and local configurations.”，而权限页逐字为 “For security, `autoMode` configuration is read only from your own global settings. Workspace files are ignored, including the local settings file: any file under the workspace can arrive with a checkout, and a repository able to add classifier `allow` entries could bias auto mode toward approving dangerous actions.”，两处对 local settings 的说法互相矛盾、核对日期未由官方统一；软指引逐字为 “These rules are **soft guidance** — injected into the classifier prompt as reference; the final decision is still made by the AI classifier.”；用户级 `AGENTS.md` 逐字为 “The user-level `AGENTS.md` is also injected into the classifier context, as evidence of what the session was asked to accomplish. Only the user-level file is read. Its content describes intent and does not constitute authorization: an action it appears to sanction is still blocked when it conflicts with a safety rule.”；裁决模型官方文档从不点名、只写 “the AI classifier”，唯一线索是发布说明 1.1.63（2026-09-24）逐字 “Approval requests in Auto permission mode now follow the BYOK model”，另有 1.1.62（2026-09-23）“Improved the two-phase review flow of Auto permission mode to speed up reviews”、1.1.56（2026-09-18）“Improved Auto mode: when the auto-approval component is temporarily unavailable, it now falls back to user confirmation and shows the reason”、1.1.48（2026-09-09）与 1.1.40（2026-09-01）两条，其中 1.1.56 的「回退用户确认」与当前权限页的「拒绝而不是提示」不一致、按文档页记录；1.1.64（2026-09-25）到最新 1.1.72（2026-10-15）九条发布说明没有任何 auto 模式、审批、分类器或权限条目，本字段自 2026-09-24 起没有新的官方增量；`/cli/model`、`/cli/custom-models`、`/cli/working-modes`、`/cli/how-it-works` 与 SDK 权限页都没有说明哪个模型做裁决，官方也没有公布分类器超时、token 预算、温度或裁决审计日志；Qoder CLI 没有 `/auto` 斜杠命令，`--yolo` 与 `--dangerously-skip-permissions` 都等于 `--permission-mode bypass_permissions`）、Provider 与 BYOK（2026-10-17 复核官方 Models 页、Custom Models 页与 CLI Release Notes 并补录：`/model` 有 Default、New Models、Custom 三个页签、`Tab` 与 `Shift+Tab` 切换，Default 是四个分档模型池（Smart Routing (Auto) ~1.0x、Ultimate ~1.6x、Performance ~1.1x、Efficient ~0.3x，官方注明倍率是计费系数而不是固定 Credit 数，实际用量还取决于输入输出长度、缓存命中、thinking effort、上下文窗口与工具调用），New Models 列出当前可直选的具体模型并写 “Currently, only some models are open for direct selection.”、可用模型与 `price_factor` 随服务端模型列表变化而以 `/model` 界面和 `--list-models` 输出为准；Custom 页签逐字为 “Connect models from your own provider subscriptions. Qoder supports a curated list of providers. Configure your API key to start using them.”，Models 页点名的 Supported Providers 为 Alibaba Cloud Model Studio、DeepSeek、Z.ai、Kimi、MiniMax、Xiaomi MIMO，而 Custom Models 页把同一栏写成 “Refer to the live catalog displayed by the Custom wizard.”，两页口径不同、本次按「以向导实时目录为准 + Models 页点名六家」并列记录；Custom Models 页另逐字要求 “Configure BYOK through the Custom wizard in `/model`. Do not configure BYOK manually in `settings.json`; the available providers, models, and credential fields are determined by the BYOK catalog available to the current account.”，并给出添加流程（Custom 页签 → `Add custom model...` → Provider → model type → model → 输入 API Key 与其余信息 → “After validation succeeds, the configuration is saved automatically and the model is immediately available for selection.”）、删除方式（Custom 页签选中后按 `d`）与三条故障排查（页签或添加入口缺失时确认当前账号与计划是否可用 BYOK、某厂商或模型缺失时以向导显示的目录为准、API Key 校验失败时检查 Key 有效性与订阅计划是否授予该模型访问权）；Available edition 为 Individual plan；BYOK 时间线为 0.1.31（2026-03-13）“Support for custom models with providers like Alibaba Cloud Bailian Coding Plan”、0.2.0（2026-04-27）“Multi-model support … with BYOK so you're no longer locked to a single provider.”、0.2.5（2026-04-29）“BYOK Custom URL: bring your own API key with custom endpoint support”、1.1.50（2026-09-11）“Personal edition users can now centrally configure and manage BYOK models with custom URL endpoints from the Custom page in `/model`”、1.1.63（2026-09-24）“Approval requests in Auto permission mode now follow the BYOK model”，1.1.64（2026-09-25）至 1.1.72（2026-10-15，核对日期最新版本）十个版本里只有 1.1.67 的模型信息展示、1.1.70 的 Bailian 模型请求限流自动重试与 1.1.71 的模型列表加载体验三条与模型相关、没有任何 Provider、BYOK 或自定义端点条目；启动旗标 `--model`、`--reasoning-effort`、`--context-window` 只作用于当前会话且不持久化，`qoder --list-models` 不开 TUI 直接打印当前账号可用模型，TUI 里按 `Enter` 选中的模型自动写入 `~/.qoder/settings.json` 并跨会话保留而按 `s` 只对当前会话生效；0.2.5 与 1.1.50 的「自定义 URL 端点」只出现在变更记录里，Models 页与 Custom Models 页都没有描述填写自定义 base URL 的字段，因此 `model-compatible-endpoint` 字段的 Qoder 结论「公开文档仅列指定厂商；无任意 URL」本次未处理、留待后续单独核对、Subagent 后台与并行（2026-10-18 复核官方 Subagent 页、Parallel Tasks 页、设置参考与 CLI Release Notes 后补录：frontmatter `background` 行逐字为 “Whether the Subagent should launch in the background by default. Requires background Subagent sessions to be enabled.”、类型 Boolean、必填 No，官方没有给出该字段的默认值；后台行为逐字为 “When background Subagent sessions are enabled, independent work can also run in the background and notify the main session later.” 与 “When a Subagent runs in the background, the main session first receives a launch result and can continue work that does not depend on it. If there is no other work, the main conversation shows a waiting state to indicate that the session is still running. When the task finishes, its result automatically returns to the main session for synthesis or follow-up work.”；并行为 “Qoder CLI can dispatch multiple independent Subagents in parallel.” 与 “Yes. Qoder CLI can dispatch independent Subagents concurrently.”，Parallel Tasks 页把 Background Agents / Tasks 与 Worktree、Subagents 并列为三种隔离方式（“using **Background Agents / Tasks** to run multiple subtasks in parallel”）并给出 “Use `/tasks` to view currently running background tasks:” 与 “For a single complex task, you can break it down and delegate parts to multiple Subagents for parallel processing.”；查看与停止为 “In TUI, use `/tasks` to view its status and result or to stop it while it is still running.”；启用后台 Subagent 会话的开关记为未确认——设置参考全页没有任何含 background 的设置键（`agents.background`、`backgroundSubagents` 与 `general.background` 都不存在）、没有 Subagent 并发数或最大并发键、也没有 `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS`，`agents` 组只有 `agents.overrides`（object、默认 `{}`、Restart 为 Yes、“Override configuration by Agent name (enablement, tools, models, run limits, etc.). For fields, see Subagent.”），Subagent 页与 Parallel Tasks 页也都没有说明如何启用；并发上限只见于 CLI Release Notes 的 1.1.31（2026-08-26）“Added a concurrency limit for subagents, configurable via the `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENT_SUBAGENTS` environment variable”、该条没有给出默认并发数，同页相关条目还有 0.1.43（2026-04-16）“Implemented permission request queuing and sequential display for concurrent subagents”、0.2.2（2026-04-28）“Fixed /tasks command unable to exit”、1.1.17（2026-08-07）“Fixed the subagent display in the TUI task panel”、1.1.42（2026-09-03）“Limited subagent spawning to one nested level by default” 与 1.1.55（2026-09-17）“Fixed concurrent configuration updates in Qoder CLI overwriting each other”，全页没有含 “background subagents”、“background sessions” 或 “parallel subagents” 字样的条目；`background` 只能写在 Markdown 定义里、`--agents` 的 JSON schema 不支持该字段；Subagent 页只描述 TUI、Headless Mode 与 Non-Interactive 三种 CLI 用法，没有 ACP、SDK 或 Cloud 的前后台差异；公开 CLI Release Notes 最新条目已由此前记录的 CLI 1.1.72（2026-10-15）更新为 CLI 1.1.73（2026-10-16））、生命周期 Hooks 的执行顺序与并发复核（2026-10-19 复核官方 Hooks 页 `docs.qoder.com/cli/hooks`：全页没有任何说明同一事件的多个 Hook 并行还是顺序执行的语句，也没有并发数字，与顺序相关的只有配置合并口径 “All three sources are loaded and merged together (hooks for the same event do not override each other)”；组级 `async`、条目级 `async`（“this single hook runs in the background; overrides the group-level `async`”）与 `asyncRewake` 都没有并发上限，事件表仍是 23 个，四类 Handler 的默认超时仍是 command 600、http 600、prompt 30、agent 60 秒；公开 Release Notes 最新条目为 CLI 1.1.73（2026-10-16，只有 “Added support for using site capabilities via qoder sites subcommands”），1.1.68（2026-10-10）至 1.1.73 六版没有任何 Hook 相关条目，因此同事件的执行顺序与并发数记为未确认）、压缩与发布说明复核（2026-10-20 复核公开 Release Notes，最新条目已为 CLI 1.1.74（2026-10-19，小节标题 “Faster, More Reliable Session Management and Image-to-Image Generation”，三条逐字为 “Improved the execution speed of session forking and the /branch command”、“File edits and writes no longer fail when a checkpoint backup can't be created, and rewinding now restores every file it can and clearly lists any files that couldn't be restored” 与 “Added image-to-image support for the ImageGen tool”），1.1.71（2026-10-14，模型列表加载与更新体验）至 1.1.74 四段没有压缩阈值或空闲触发条目，上一条 Hook 段记录的「最新条目为 CLI 1.1.73」随之过期；官方 Undo and Restore 页（`docs.qoder.com/cli/undo-restore`）在 2026-10-20 复核时仍只写 “Qoder CLI records the file editing history during a session”、“Rewind only applies to the editing history recorded within the current session” 与 “File changes made through manual editing or Shell commands may not be reverted by Rewind”，没有描述检查点备份失败或 `/rewind` 部分恢复的行为；1.1.74 的检查点备份失败不再阻断写入与 `/rewind` 部分恢复属检查点与回退字段、本次未处理）、会话命名（2026-10-21 复核官方 Slash 命令参考、Session Management 页（1,174,731 字节）与 Release Notes（1,706,864 字节）后更正一处过期记录：此前记为「当前 TUI 内置命令表未列出会话重命名或标题命令」并把整列证据状态标为未确认，现在 Slash 命令参考的会话管理表在 `/resume` 与 `/branch` 之间列出 `/rename`，描述逐字为 “Rename the current session.”，Session Management 页也逐字写 “You can also use `/rename` to rename the current session from within the session.” 并给出启动命名 “Use `-n` (`--name`) to set an easily recognizable display name for a session:” 加示例 `qoder -n "Refactor login"`（旗标表 `-n, --name <name>` 描述 “Set the session display name”）；Release Notes 的 CLI 1.1.52（September 14, 2026，小节 “Improved the rename and btw commands”）逐字为 “Type `/rename` to auto-generate a session name based on the current conversation; manually entered names are automatically sanitized and length-limited”，即不带参数时按当前对话自动生成名称、手工输入的名称会被净化并限制长度，CLI 1.1.9（July 30, 2026，小节 “Rename, Skill Scanning, and Startup Improvements”）逐字为 “Added support for running /rename while a task is in progress”；同页写明恢复只按会话 ID——“Use `-r` (`--resume`) to resume a previous session by its ID:” 加示例 `qoder -r <session-id>`、旗标表 `-r, --resume [id]` 描述 “Resume a specific session; opens the Session Browser if no ID is provided”，没有按名称恢复或把名称当作其他子命令句柄的说明；存储位置只给出会话日志 `~/.qoder/projects/<processed-project-path-name>/<session-id>.jsonl` 与状态 `<session-id>/state.json` 并写 “the system automatically generates a session ID”，没有写明显示名保存在哪个文件或字段，长度上限与净化规则也只写 “length-limited” 与 “sanitized” 而没有数字或规则，这三项仍记为未确认；官方 SDK 参考页（2026-10-21 抓取，1,003,825 字节）全文没有 rename 字样、也没有 `session_title` 一类字符串，此前「SDK 消息类型可通知宿主会话标题发生变化」无法从该来源复核，本次删除；Qoder 列证据状态由「未确认」改为「官方确认」，CLI 1.1.4（July 23, 2026，小节 “Background Tasks, Terminal Titles, and Input Enhancements”）的 “Improved terminal session window title updates” 属终端窗口标题不计入本字段；核对日期 Release Notes 最新条目已由此前记录的 CLI 1.1.74（October 19, 2026）更新为 CLI 1.1.75（October 20, 2026，小节 “Configurable session cleanup and faster /branch command”，两条逐字为 “Removed automatic session cleanup; it can now be enabled by modifying the configuration” 与 “Improved the execution speed of the /branch command”），1.1.71 至 1.1.75 五段都没有会话命名条目）、OS 级文件系统沙箱文档状态（2026-10-21 复核并更正：官方文档索引 `https://docs.qoder.com/llms.txt`（646 个链接，其中 96 个为 `/cli/` 页面）里没有 CLI 的 Sandbox 页，`https://docs.qoder.com/en/cli/sandbox` 与 `https://docs.qoder.com/cli/sandbox` 都返回 404；逐页抓取这 96 个 `/cli/*.md` 后只有三页出现 sandbox 字样——`cli/sdk/errors` 的退出码表把 `44` 一行逐字记为 “Fatal sandbox error”、`cli/sdk/how-it-works` 的 “`cwd`, tool allowlists, permission rules, hooks, sandboxing, and infrastructure isolation are complementary controls. Configure them according to the consequences of the task.” 与 `cli/sdk/skills` 的 “Don't treat `skills` as a sandbox: security boundaries belong to tool allow/deny lists, the permission callback, permission modes, and sandboxing combined.”；设置参考页（30,253 字节的 Markdown 形式）全文没有 sandbox 字样，与文件边界相关的键只有 `permissions.allow`、`permissions.ask`、`permissions.deny`、`permissions.additionalDirectories` 与 `permissions.trustDirectories`，SDK 参考页也没有 `sandbox.filesystem` 一类键，因此本仓库此前记录的「SDK 的 `sandbox.filesystem` 可设置 allow/deny read/write，且 Sandbox 默认关闭」在核对日期已无法从官方文档复核、改为未确认；索引里含 sandbox 的其余链接属 Qoder IDE 与 Cloud Agents 两个别的 Surface，按本仓库规则不并入 CLI；该次复核时 Release Notes 最新条目为 CLI 1.1.75（October 20, 2026），1.1.66 至 1.1.75 各段都没有本地沙箱键或 OS 隔离条目，整份发布说明唯一含 sandbox 字样的是 CLI 1.0.0（May 20, 2026）段 “Enterprise Integration” 下的 Cloud Agents 一条）、命令行参考与 Release Notes 复核（2026-10-22：官方命令行参考的子命令表共 14 行——`mcp`、`plugins`（别名 `plugin`）、`skills`（别名 `skill`）、`hooks`（别名 `hook`）、`agents`（别名 `agent`）、`login`、`commit`、`rollback`、`update`、`remote-control`、`status`、`sites`、`feedback` 与 `wiki`，其中与常驻服务有关的只有 “`remote-control` | Start the remote-control daemon.” 一行，`status` 一行是 “Display session status.” 而不是 daemon 状态，表里没有任何 daemon 的 stop、restart、logs 或 update 子命令；`--acp` 在 “Editor Integration” 段逐字为 “Start as an ACP server for integration via standard input/output by clients implementing the ACP protocol, such as Zed.”；Release Notes 最新条目已推进到 CLI 1.1.77（October 22, 2026，小节 “Improved Session Reliability and More Slash Commands While the Agent Responds”），其前的 1.1.76（October 21, 2026，“Performance optimization”）、1.1.75、1.1.74（October 19, 2026）、1.1.73（October 16, 2026）与 1.1.72（October 15, 2026）五版连同 1.1.77 都没有 daemon、remote-control 或后台服务条目）、项目目录信任（2026-10-23 复核官方 Permissions 的 “Trust Directories” 与 “Extending Trust” 两节、Settings reference、CLI Commands and Parameters、Slash 命令参考与 Release Notes 并补录：权限页逐字为 “Qoder treats the startup current working directory (CWD) as the **main trust directory**. Within trusted directories:” 加三条 “File reads are allowed by default”、“File writes can be auto-approved in `accept_edits` and `auto` modes”、“Non-default permission modes (auto, bypass, etc.) are allowed to take effect”，随后 “If the current directory is not trusted, Qoder forces a fallback to `default` mode.”，权限模式一节另写 “Non-default modes only take effect in trusted directories. If the current directory is not trusted, Qoder falls back to `default`.”，`bypass_permissions`（YOLO）行适用场景为 “Trusted local experiments only” 且 “Skips approval prompts. A small set of path-shape protections stays active”；扩展信任逐字为 “Add additional trusted working directories via `--add-dir`, the `/add-dir` command, or `permissions.additionalDirectories`:” 加示例 `qoder --add-dir ../shared` 与 `{"permissions": {"additionalDirectories": ["../shared"]}}`，以及 “You can also configure `permissions.trustDirectories` in global settings to permanently trust frequently-used directories.”；**新增记录键** `security.folderTrust.enabled`——boolean、默认 `true`、Restart 为 Yes、描述 “Enable Folder Trust.”，本仓库此前未记录该键，官方没有写它设为 `false` 时的行为因而该取值的效果记为未确认；`permissions.trustDirectories` 为 string[]、默认 `[]`、Restart 为 No、描述 “Directories explicitly trusted by the user; stored in user-level configuration and not overridden by project-level configuration.”，`permissions.additionalDirectories` 为 string[]、默认 `[]`、Restart 为 No、描述 “Additional trusted directories added to workspace context (corresponding to `/add-dir`).”，另有 `context.loadMemoryFromIncludeDirectories`（boolean、默认 `false`、“Load memory from Additional trusted directories.”）与同组的 `security.disableYoloMode`（boolean、默认 `false`、“Disable bypass_permissions (YOLO) Permission Mode.”）；CLI 参考里含 trust 的启动参数只有 `--add-dir`（string[]、“Add Additional trusted directories for the current session (can be used multiple times).”），Slash 参考里只有 `/add-dir`（“Add trusted directories.”），两页都没有信任总开关、`/trust` 一类命令或跳过信任检查的旗标；Release Notes 的目录信任时间线为 CLI 1.0.17（June 10, 2026，小节 “Agent Sessions, Permissions Upgrade & SOCKS5 Proxy”）“Upgraded /permissions: directory completion, workspace dialog, scope-colored trust tags, and remove confirmation”、CLI 1.0.15（June 9, 2026）“Fixed project permission rules not taking effect immediately after granting workspace trust”、CLI 0.2.9（May 8, 2026）“Fixed /add-dir trusted directories still requiring read permission approval” 与同版 “Fixed symlink reads bypassing permission boundary checks”，三条都没有给出信任记录的保存文件或 `security.folderTrust.enabled` 的引入版本，官方权限页也没有描述 1.0.17 提到的 workspace dialog，因此首次信任决定的保存位置与该对话框字段记为未确认；核对日期 Release Notes 最新条目仍为 CLI 1.1.77（October 22, 2026），1.1.72 至 1.1.77 六版都没有目录信任条目） |

产品更新速度较快，因此这里用“核对日期 + Surface + 来源”定位内容。版本号只在证据明确对应某一发行版时写入单条记录。

## 证据状态

| 状态 | 判断条件 |
| --- | --- |
| 官方确认 | 官方文档、官方仓库、Release 或 Changelog 直接描述该功能 |
| 源码确认 | 公开源码能定位入口、配置或行为 |
| 运行确认 | 在记录版本、系统和 Surface 中实际执行 |
| 条件项 | 只在特定系统、账号、模式、功能开关或 Surface 中成立 |
| 未确认 | 当前一手材料不足，或不同一手材料冲突 |

## Surface 规则

每条结论至少绑定到以下信息：

```text
产品 + 日期或版本 + Surface + 来源
```

CLI、IDE、桌面端、Web、Cloud 和 SDK 分开判断。某个 Surface 出现功能，不代表其他 Surface 自动具备相同命令、权限边界或会话行为。

## 更新规则

1. 先更新对应 Markdown 表格和网页数据。
2. 命令新增、删除或改名时，更新对照表和完整命令目录；不能只扫描命令实现目录，还要核对硬编码命令、bundled Skill、用户/项目/扩展 Skill、Markdown/TOML 命令、保存的 Workflow 和 MCP Prompt 加载器。
3. Subagent 行为变化时，拆分到上下文、权限、工具、后台、嵌套或 Worktree 对应行。
4. 无法从一手资料确认时保留“未确认”，不根据相似产品推断。
5. 每次影响矩阵内容的修改写入 [`updates/`](../updates/)。
